kendalltau, spearmanr\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# NER evaluation (strict match)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef entity_strict_match(\\n    pred_entities: list[dict],\\n    gold_entities: list[dict],\\n) -> tuple[int, int, int]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute strict entity match counts.\\n\\n    Strict match requires exact span (start, end) AND exact type.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        pred_entities: Predicted entity dicts with keys text, type, start, end.\\n        gold_entities: Gold entity dicts with the same schema.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (tp, fp, fn) counts.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pred_set = {(e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in pred_entities}\\n    gold_set = {(e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in gold_entities}\\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\\n    fp = len(pred_set - gold_set)\\n    fn = len(gold_set - pred_set)\\n    return tp, fp, fn\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ner_f1(\\n    predictions: list[dict],\\n    golds: list[dict],\\n) -> dict[str, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute micro-averaged NER precision, recall, and F1.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        predictions: List of Extraction dicts (one per instance).\\n        golds: List of Extraction dicts (one per instance), aligned with predictions.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Dict with keys \\\"precision\\\", \\\"recall\\\", \\\"f1\\\".\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    total_tp = total_fp = total_fn = 0\\n    for total_tp += tp\\n        total_fp += fp\\n        total_fn += fn\\n    return _prf(total_tp, total_fp, total_fn)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# EAE evaluation (argument match)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef total_tp += tp\\n        total_fp += fp\\n        total_fn += fn\\n    return _prf(total_tp, total_fp, total_fn)\\n\\n\\n# gold_set)\\n    fp = len(pred_set - gold_set)\\n    fn = len(gold_set - pred_set)\\n    return tp, fp, fn\\n\\n\\ndef compute_re_f1(\\n    predictions: list[dict],\\n    golds: list[dict],\\n) -> return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef load_instances(path, n_samples=8):\\n    instances = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            inst = json.loads(line)\\n            inst[\\\"samples\\\"] = inst[\\\"samples\\\"][:n_samples]\\n            instances.append(inst)\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef per_instance_entity_f1(pred_entities, gold_entities):\\n    tp, fp, fn = entity_strict_match(pred_entities, gold_entities)\\n    if tp + fp == 0 and tp + fn == 0:\\n        return 1.0\\n    if tp == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    p = tp / (tp + fp)\\n    r = tp / (tp + fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef dgs_select(inst, n_samples=8):\\n    gold_ents = inst[\\\"gold\\\"][\\\"entities\\\"]\\n    samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"][:n_samples]\\n    greedy = inst[\\\"greedy\\\"]\\n\\n    sample_f1s = [per_instance_entity_f1(s[\\\"entities\\\"], gold_ents) for s in samples]\\n    is_degenerate = len(set(round(f, 10) for f in sample_f1s)) == 1\\n\\n    lp_idx = max(range(len(samples)), key=lambda i: samples[i][\\\"mean_logprob\\\"])\\n\\n    if is_degenerate:\\n        selected_ents = greedy[\\\"entities\\\"]\\n        method = \\\"greedy\\\"\\n    else:\\n        selected_ents = samples[lp_idx][\\\"entities\\\"]\\n        method = \\\"lp\\\"\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"is_degenerate\\\": is_degenerate,\\n        \\\"greedy_f1\\\": \\\"greedy_entities\\\": greedy[\\\"entities\\\"],\\n        \\\"lp_entities\\\": samples[lp_idx][\\\"entities\\\"],\\n        \\\"gold_entities\\\": gold_ents,\\n        \\\"method\\\": method,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef micro_f1_from_entity_lists(pred_list, gold_list):\\n    total_tp = total_fp = total_fn = 0\\n    for pred, gold in zip(pred_list, gold_list):\\n        tp, fp, fn = r = total_tp / (total_tp + total_fn)\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_ci(values, n_boot=10000, seed=42):\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    arr = np.array(values)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return {\\\"mean\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lo\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_hi\\\": 0.0}\\n    boot_means = np.array([arr[rng.randint(0, n, n)].mean() for _ in range(n_boot)])\\n 0:\\n        return {\\\"delta\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lo\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_hi\\\": 0.0, \\\"significant\\\": False}\\n    deltas = []\\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n a_arr - b_arr\\n    n = len(diff)\\n    count = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        signs = rng.choice([-1, 1], size=n)\\n        if abs((diff * signs).mean()) >= abs(observed):\\n            count += 1\\n    return {\\\"observed_delta\\\": float(observed), \\\"p_value\\\": float(count / n_perm)}\\n\\n\\ndef analyze_dataset(instances, name, n_samples, n_boot, n_perm, seed):\\n    results = n_boot)]),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_delta_ci(a, b, n_boot=10000, seed=42):\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    a_arr, b_arr = np.array(a), np.array(b)\\n    n = len(a_arr)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return {\\\"delta\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lo\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_hi\\\": 0.0, \\\"significant\\\": False}\\n    deltas = []\\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n        deltas.append(a_arr[idx].mean() - b_arr[idx].mean())\\n    deltas = sorted(deltas)\\n    lo = deltas[int(0.025 * n_boot)]\\n    hi = deltas[int(0.975 * n_boot)]\\n    return {\\n        \\\"delta\\\": float((a_arr - b_arr).mean()),\\n        \\\"ci_lo\\\": float(lo),\\n        \\\"ci_hi\\\": float(hi),\\n        \\\"significant\\\": bool(lo > 0 or hi < 0),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef paired_permutation_test(a, b, n_perm=10000, seed=42):\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    a_arr, b_arr = np.array(a), np.array(b)\\n    observed = a_arr.mean() - b_arr.mean()\\n    diff = a_arr - b_arr\\n    n = len(diff)\\n    count = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        signs = rng.choice([-1, 1], size=n)\\n        if abs((diff * signs).mean()) >= abs(observed):\\n            count += 1\\n    return {\\\"observed_delta\\\": float(observed), \\\"p_value\\\": float(count / n_perm)}\\n\\n\\ndef analyze_dataset(instances, name, n_samples, n_boot, n_perm, seed):\\n    results = range(n) if j != i)\\n        scores.append(total / (n - 1))\\n    return scores\\n\\n\\ndef entity_f1(pred_sample, gold):\\n    pred = span_set(pred_sample)\\n    gold_s = span_set(gold)\\n    if len(pred) == 0 and len(gold_s) == 0:\\n        return 1.0\\n    if len(pred) == 0 or len(gold_s) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    tp = len(pred & gold_s)\\n    p = tp / len(pred)\\n    r = tp / len(gold_s)\\n    if p + r == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\\n\\n\\ndef degeneracy_ratio(samples):\\n    sets = [text_type_set(s) for s in samples]\\n    n_unique = len(set(sets))\\n    return n_unique / len(samples)\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_dataset(name, cfg):\\n    instances = load_data(cfg[\\\"path\\\"], cfg[\\\"max_samples\\\"], cfg[\\\"gold_filter\\\"])\\n    n = len(instances)\\n\\n    greedy_f1s, lp_f1s, mbr_f1s, oracle_f1s = [], [], [], []\\n    degen_ratios = []\\n\\n    for inst in instances:\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n        logprobs = inst[\\\"logprobs\\\"]\\n\\n        greedy_f1s.append(entity_f1(inst[\\\"greedy\\\"], gold))\\n\\n        if len(logprobs) == len(samples):\\n            lp_idx = max(range(len(logprobs)), key=lambda i: logprobs[i])\\n        else:\\n            lp_idx = 0\\n        lp_f1s.append(entity_f1(samples[lp_idx], gold))\\n\\n        scores = mbr_scores(samples)\\n        mbr_idx = scores.index(max(scores))\\n        mbr_f1s.append(entity_f1(samples[mbr_idx], gold))\\n\\n        best_f1 = max(entity_f1(s, gold) for s in samples)\\n        oracle_f1s.append(best_f1)\\n\\n        degen_ratios.append(degeneracy_ratio(samples))\\n\\n    greedy_avg = sum(greedy_f1s) / n\\n    lp_avg = sum(lp_f1s) / n\\n    mbr_avg = sum(mbr_f1s) / n\\n    oracle_avg = sum(oracle_f1s) / n\\n\\n    degen_groups = {\\\"low_diversity\\\": [], \\\"medium\\\": [], \\\"high_diversity\\\": []}\\n    for i, dr in enumerate(degen_ratios):\\n        if dr <= 0.25:\\n            degen_groups[\\\"low_diversity\\\"].append(i)\\n        elif dr <= 0.625:\\n            degen_groups[\\\"medium\\\"].append(i)\\n        else:\\n            degen_groups[\\\"high_diversity\\\"].append(i)\\n\\n    degen_results = {}\\n    for group_name, indices in degen_groups.items():\\n        if not indices:\\n            degen_results[group_name] = None\\n            continue\\n        gn = len(indices)\\n        degen_results[group_name] = {\\n            \\\"count\\\": gn,\\n            \\\"greedy\\\": sum(greedy_f1s[i] for i in indices) / gn,\\n            \\\"lp\\\": sum(lp_f1s[i] for i in indices) / gn,\\n            \\\"mbr\\\": sum(mbr_f1s[i] for i in indices) / gn,\\n            \\\"oracle\\\": sum(oracle_f1s[i] for i in indices) / gn,\\n        }\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"name\\\": name, \\\"n\\\": n,\\n        \\\"greedy\\\": greedy_avg, \\\"lp\\\": lp_avg,\\n        \\\"mbr\\\": mbr_avg, \\\"oracle\\\": oracle_avg,\\n        \\\"degen\\\": degen_results,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    results = []\\n    for name, cfg in DATASETS.items():\\n        r = evaluate_dataset(name, cfg)\\n        results.append(r)\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*60}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"Dataset: {r['name']} ({r['n']} instances, N={cfg['max_samples']})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'='*60}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Greedy F1:        {r['greedy']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  LP Selection F1:  {r['lp']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  MBR Selection F1: {r['mbr']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Oracle F1:        {r['oracle']:.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Delta (MBR-Greedy): {r['mbr'] - r['greedy']:+.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"  Delta (MBR-LP):     {r['mbr'] - r['lp']:+.4f}\\\")\\n\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n  Degeneracy breakdown:\\\")\\n        for gname in [\\\"low_diversity\\\", \\\"medium\\\", \\\"high_diversity\\\"]:\\n            d = r[\\\"degen\\\"][gname]\\n            if d is None:\\n                print(f\\\"    {gname}: (no instances)\\\")\\n            else:\\n                print(f\\\"    {gname} ({d['count']} inst): \\\"\\n                      f\\\"Greedy={d['greedy']:.4f}  LP={d['lp']:.4f}  \\\"\\n                      f\\\"MBR={d['mbr']:.4f}  Oracle={d['oracle']:.4f}  \\\"\\n                      f\\\"MBR-Grd={d['mbr']-d['greedy']:+.4f}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n\\\\n{'='*70}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"Summary Table\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'='*70}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'Dataset':<12} {'Greedy':>8} {'LP Sel':>8} {'MBR Sel':>8} {'Oracle':>8} {'MBR-Grd':>8} {'MBR-LP':>8}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'-'*12} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\\\")\\n    for r in results:\\n        print(f\\\"{r['name']:<12} {r['greedy']:>8.4f} {r['lp']:>8.4f} {r['mbr']:>8.4f} \\\"\\n              f\\\"{r['oracle']:>8.4f} {r['mbr']-r['greedy']:>+8.4f} {r['mbr']-r['lp']:>+8.4f}\\\")\\n\\n    # {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"exp-014: Entity/relation majority voting as selection strategy + confidence signal.\\n\\nFor each instance's N=8 samples:\\n1. Count (text, type) frequency for NER, (head, tail, type) for RE\\n2. Majority voting: keep items appearing > N/2 times\\n3. Compute consensus F1 vs gold\\n4. Compare against greedy, oracle, SJ-selected strategies\\n5. Compute voting_confidence = mean(voting_rate of consensus items)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr, kendalltau\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, '/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code')\\nfrom consistency import compute_all_consistency_scores\\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1, entity_strict_match, relation_strict_match\\n\\nDATA_PATH = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/mvp_pilot_004/samples.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(path):\\n    instances = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                instances.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef auroc_simple(scores, labels):\\n    scores = np.array(scores, dtype=float)\\n    labels = np.array(labels, dtype=int)\\n    if len(set(labels)) < 2:\\n        return float('nan')\\n    pos = scores[labels == 1]\\n    neg = scores[labels == 0]\\n    n_pos, n_neg = len(pos), len(neg)\\n    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0:\\n        return float('nan')\\n    count = 0.0\\n    for p in pos:\\n        count += np.sum(p > neg) + 0.5 * np.sum(p == neg)\\n    return count / (n_pos * n_neg)\\n\\n\\ndef majority_vote_entities(samples, threshold_frac=0.5):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build consensus entity set via majority voting.\\n    \\n    Returns:\\n        consensus_entities: list of entity dicts (with best representative span)\\n        voting_rates: dict mapping (text, type) -> fraction of samples containing it\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    threshold = N * threshold_frac\\n    \\n    # Count (text, type) occurrences and track spans\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    span_counter = {}  # (text, type) -> Counter of (start, end)\\n    span_to_entity = {}  # (text, type, start, end) -> entity dict\\n    \\n    for s in samples:\\n        for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n            key = (e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"])\\n            counter[key] += 1\\n            if key not in span_counter:\\n                span_counter[key] = Counter()\\n            span = (e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"])\\n            span_counter[key][span] += 1\\n            span_to_entity[(key[0], key[1], span[0], span[1])] = e\\n    \\n    # Build consensus: items with count > threshold\\n    consensus = []\\n    voting_rates = {}\\n    for key, count in counter.items():\\n        rate = count / N\\n        voting_rates[key] = rate\\n        if count > threshold:\\n            # Pick most common span\\n            best_span = span_counter[key].most_common(1)[0][0]\\n            entity = span_to_entity[(key[0], key[1], best_span[0], best_span[1])]\\n            consensus.append(entity)\\n    \\n    return consensus, voting_rates\\n\\n\\ndef majority_vote_relations(samples, threshold_frac=0.5):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build consensus relation set via majority voting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    threshold = N * threshold_frac\\n    \\n    counter = Counter()\\n    span_counter = {}\\n    span_to_rel = {}\\n    \\n    for s in samples:\\n        for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", []):\\n            key = (r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"])\\n            counter[key] += 1\\n            if key not in span_counter:\\n                span_counter[key] = Counter()\\n            span = (r[\\\"head_start\\\"], r[\\\"head_end\\\"], r[\\\"tail_start\\\"], r[\\\"tail_end\\\"])\\n            span_counter[key][span] += 1\\n            span_to_rel[(key[0], key[1], key[2]) + span] = r\\n    \\n    consensus = []\\n    voting_rates = {}\\n    for key, count in counter.items():\\n        rate = count / N\\n        voting_rates[key] = rate\\n        if count > threshold:\\n            best_span = span_counter[key].most_common(1)[0][0]\\n            rel = span_to_rel[key + best_span]\\n            consensus.append(rel)\\n    \\n    return consensus, voting_rates\\n\\n\\ndef voting_confidence(voting_rates, consensus_keys):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mean voting rate of consensus items.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not consensus_keys:\\n        return 0.0\\n    rates = [voting_rates[k] for k in consensus_keys]\\n    return float(np.mean(rates))\\n\\n\\ndef analyze(instances, subtask):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Full analysis for one subtask.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    field_check = \\\"entities\\\" if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else \\\"relations\\\"\\n    vote_fn = majority_vote_entities if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else majority_vote_relations\\n    \\n    # Per-instance metrics\\n    greedy_f1s = []\\n    voting_f1s = []\\n    oracle_f1s = []\\n    voting_confs = []\\n    \\n    # Consistency scores\\n    consistency = compute_all_consistency_scores(instances, subtask=subtask)\\n    sj = consistency[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"]\\n    fk = consistency[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"]\\n    oracle_indices = consistency[\\\"oracle_indices\\\"]\\n    \\n    # SJ-selected: pick sample with behavior closest to consensus\\n    # Actually, we'll pick sample with highest SJ score... but SJ is per-instance, not per-sample.\\n    # Instead, compute per-sample: pick the sample whose individual F1 is highest (oracle).\\n    # For \\\"SJ-oracle\\\": we pick the sample that SJ would rank highest.\\n    # But SJ is a single number per instance. The natural \\\"SJ selection\\\" doesn't pick a sample.\\n    # Let's compute: for each sample, its avg pairwise SJ with other samples, pick the highest.\\n    \\n    for i, inst in enumerate(instances):\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n        greedy = inst.get(\\\"greedy\\\", {\\\"entities\\\": [], \\\"relations\\\": []})\\n        \\n        # Greedy F1\\n        greedy_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, gold, subtask))\\n        \\n        # Oracle F1 (best of N)\\n        sample_f1s = [per_instance_f1(s, gold, subtask) for s in samples]\\n        oracle_f1s.append(max(sample_f1s) if sample_f1s else 0.0)\\n        \\n        # Majority voting\\n        consensus_items, v_rates = vote_fn(samples)\\n        consensus_extraction = {\\n            \\\"entities\\\": consensus_items if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else [],\\n            \\\"relations\\\": consensus_items if subtask == \\\"re\\\" else [],\\n        }\\n        voting_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(consensus_extraction, gold, subtask))\\n        \\n        # Voting confidence\\n        consensus_keys = set()\\n        if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n            consensus_keys = {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in consensus_items}\\n        else:\\n            consensus_keys = {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in consensus_items}\\n        \\n        # Use mean voting rate over ALL unique items (not just consensus) as signal\\n        if v_rates:\\n            voting_confs.append(float(np.mean(list(v_rates.values()))))\\n        else:\\n            voting_confs.append(0.0)\\n    \\n    # Filter: gold-nonempty\\n    valid = [i for i, inst in enumerate(instances) if inst[\\\"gold\\\"].get(field_check)]\\n    n_gold_empty = len(instances) - len(valid)\\n    \\n    f1_v = [greedy_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    vf1_v = [voting_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    of1_v = [oracle_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    vc_v = [voting_confs[i] for i in valid]\\n    sj_v = [sj[i] for i in valid]\\n    fk_v = [fk[i] for i in valid]\\n    \\n    # Median-split AUROC labels (fallback to >= when > yields single-class labels)\\n    median_f1 = float(np.median(f1_v))\\n    labels_v = [1 if f > median_f1 else 0 for f in f1_v]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"exp-014: Entity/relation majority voting as selection strategy + confidence signal.\\n\\nFor each instance's N=8 samples:\\n1. Count (text, type) frequency for NER, (head, tail, type) for RE\\n2. Majority voting: keep items appearing > N/2 times\\n3. Compute consensus F1 vs gold\\n4. Compare against greedy, oracle, SJ-selected strategies\\n5. Compute voting_confidence = mean(voting_rate of consensus items)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr, kendalltau\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, '/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code')\\nfrom consistency import compute_all_consistency_scores\\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1, entity_strict_match, relation_strict_match\\n\\nDATA_PATH = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/mvp_pilot_004/samples.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(path):\\n    instances = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                instances.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef auroc_simple(scores, labels):\\n    scores = np.array(scores, dtype=float)\\n    labels = np.array(labels, dtype=int)\\n    if len(set(labels)) < 2:\\n        return float('nan')\\n    pos = scores[labels == 1]\\n    neg = scores[labels == 0]\\n    n_pos, n_neg = len(pos), len(neg)\\n    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0:\\n        return float('nan')\\n    count = 0.0\\n    for p in pos:\\n        count += np.sum(p > neg) + 0.5 * np.sum(p == neg)\\n    return count / (n_pos * n_neg)\\n\\n\\ndef majority_vote_entities(samples, threshold_frac=0.5):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build consensus entity set via majority voting.\\n    \\n    Returns:\\n        consensus_entities: list of entity dicts (with best representative span)\\n        voting_rates: dict mapping (text, type) -> fraction of samples containing it\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    threshold = N * threshold_frac\\n    \\n    # Count (text, type) occurrences and track spans\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    span_counter = {}  # (text, type) -> Counter of (start, end)\\n    span_to_entity = {}  # (text, type, start, end) -> entity dict\\n    \\n    for s in samples:\\n        for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n            key = (e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"])\\n            counter[key] += 1\\n            if key not in span_counter:\\n                span_counter[key] = Counter()\\n            span = (e[\\\"start\\\"], e[\\\"end\\\"])\\n            span_counter[key][span] += 1\\n            span_to_entity[(key[0], key[1], span[0], span[1])] = e\\n    \\n    # Build consensus: items with count > threshold\\n    consensus = []\\n    voting_rates = {}\\n    for key, count in counter.items():\\n        rate = count / N\\n        voting_rates[key] = rate\\n        if count > threshold:\\n            # Pick most common span\\n            best_span = span_counter[key].most_common(1)[0][0]\\n            entity = span_to_entity[(key[0], key[1], best_span[0], best_span[1])]\\n            consensus.append(entity)\\n    \\n    return consensus, voting_rates\\n\\n\\ndef majority_vote_relations(samples, threshold_frac=0.5):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build consensus relation set via majority voting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    threshold = N * threshold_frac\\n    \\n    counter = Counter()\\n    span_counter = {}\\n    span_to_rel = {}\\n    \\n    for s in samples:\\n        for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", []):\\n            key = (r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"])\\n            counter[key] += 1\\n            if key not in span_counter:\\n                span_counter[key] = Counter()\\n            span = (r[\\\"head_start\\\"], r[\\\"head_end\\\"], r[\\\"tail_start\\\"], r[\\\"tail_end\\\"])\\n            span_counter[key][span] += 1\\n            span_to_rel[(key[0], key[1], key[2]) + span] = r\\n    \\n    consensus = []\\n    voting_rates = {}\\n    for key, count in counter.items():\\n        rate = count / N\\n        voting_rates[key] = rate\\n        if count > threshold:\\n            best_span = span_counter[key].most_common(1)[0][0]\\n            rel = span_to_rel[key + best_span]\\n            consensus.append(rel)\\n    \\n    return consensus, voting_rates\\n\\n\\ndef voting_confidence(voting_rates, consensus_keys):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mean voting rate of consensus items.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not consensus_keys:\\n        return 0.0\\n    rates = [voting_rates[k] for k in consensus_keys]\\n    return float(np.mean(rates))\\n\\n\\ndef analyze(instances, subtask):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Full analysis for one subtask.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    field_check = \\\"entities\\\" if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else \\\"relations\\\"\\n    vote_fn = majority_vote_entities if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else majority_vote_relations\\n    \\n    # Per-instance metrics\\n    greedy_f1s = []\\n    voting_f1s = []\\n    oracle_f1s = []\\n    voting_confs = []\\n    \\n    # Consistency scores\\n    consistency = compute_all_consistency_scores(instances, subtask=subtask)\\n    sj = consistency[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"]\\n    fk = consistency[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"]\\n    oracle_indices = consistency[\\\"oracle_indices\\\"]\\n    \\n    # SJ-selected: pick sample with behavior closest to consensus\\n    # Actually, we'll pick sample with highest SJ score... but SJ is per-instance, not per-sample.\\n    # Instead, compute per-sample: pick the sample whose individual F1 is highest (oracle).\\n    # For \\\"SJ-oracle\\\": we pick the sample that SJ would rank highest.\\n    # But SJ is a single number per instance. The natural \\\"SJ selection\\\" doesn't pick a sample.\\n    # Let's compute: for each sample, its avg pairwise SJ with other samples, pick the highest.\\n    \\n    for i, inst in enumerate(instances):\\n        gold = inst[\\\"gold\\\"]\\n        samples = inst[\\\"samples\\\"]\\n        greedy = inst.get(\\\"greedy\\\", {\\\"entities\\\": [], \\\"relations\\\": []})\\n        \\n        # Greedy F1\\n        greedy_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(greedy, gold, subtask))\\n        \\n        # Oracle F1 (best of N)\\n        sample_f1s = [per_instance_f1(s, gold, subtask) for s in samples]\\n        oracle_f1s.append(max(sample_f1s) if sample_f1s else 0.0)\\n        \\n        # Majority voting\\n        consensus_items, v_rates = vote_fn(samples)\\n        consensus_extraction = {\\n            \\\"entities\\\": consensus_items if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else [],\\n            \\\"relations\\\": consensus_items if subtask == \\\"re\\\" else [],\\n        }\\n        voting_f1s.append(per_instance_f1(consensus_extraction, gold, subtask))\\n        \\n        # Voting confidence\\n        consensus_keys = set()\\n        if subtask == \\\"ner\\\":\\n            consensus_keys = {(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in consensus_items}\\n        else:\\n            consensus_keys = {(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in consensus_items}\\n        \\n        # Use mean voting rate over ALL unique items (not just consensus) as signal\\n        if v_rates:\\n            voting_confs.append(float(np.mean(list(v_rates.values()))))\\n        else:\\n            voting_confs.append(0.0)\\n    \\n    # Filter: gold-nonempty\\n    valid = [i for i, inst in enumerate(instances) if inst[\\\"gold\\\"].get(field_check)]\\n    n_gold_empty = len(instances) - len(valid)\\n    \\n    f1_v = [greedy_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    vf1_v = [voting_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    of1_v = [oracle_f1s[i] for i in valid]\\n    vc_v = [voting_confs[i] for i in valid]\\n    sj_v = [sj[i] for i in valid]\\n    fk_v = [fk[i] for i in valid]\\n    \\n    # Median-split AUROC labels (fallback to >= when > yields single-class labels)\\n    median_f1 = float(np.median(f1_v))\\n    labels_v = [1 if f > median_f1 else 0 for f in f1_v]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"

span_set(pred_entities)\n    gold = span_set(gold_entities)\n    if len(pred) == 0 and len(gold) == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    if len(pred) == 0 or len(gold) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\n    if tp == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    p = tp / len(pred)\n    r = tp / len(gold)\n    return 2 * p * r / (p + r)\n\n\ndef get_sample_mean_logprob(sample, inst, idx):\n    if consensus_construct(inst, high_thresh=0.7, medium_thresh=0.3):\n    samples = inst[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n\n    entity_counts = Counter()\n    entity_sample_indices = defaultdict(list)\n\n    for i, s in enumerate(samples):\n        seen = set()\n        for e in s.get(\"entities\", []):\n            key = (e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])\n            if key not in seen:\n                entity_counts[key] += 1\n                entity_sample_indices[key].append(i)\n                seen.add(key)\n\n    sample_lps = []\n    for i, s in enumerate(samples):\n        lp = get_sample_mean_logprob(s, inst, i)\n        if lp is not None:\n            sample_lps.append(lp)\n    instance_median_lp = statistics.median(sample_lps) if sample_lps else 0.0\n\n    consensus = []\n    for entity_key, count in entity_counts.items():\n        freq = count / N\n        if freq > high_thresh:\n            consensus.append(entity_key)\n        elif freq > medium_thresh:\n            contributing_lps = []\n            for i in entity_sample_indices[entity_key]:\n                lp = get_sample_mean_logprob(samples[i], inst, i)\n                if lp is not None:\n                    contributing_lps.append(lp)\n            if contributing_lps:\n                mean_lp = statistics.mean(contributing_lps)\n                if mean_lp > threshold=0.5):\n    samples = inst[\"samples\"]\n    N = len(samples)\n    entity_counts = Counter()\n    for s in samples:\n        seen = set()\n        for e in s.get(\"entities\", []):\n            key = (e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])\n            if key not in seen:\n                entity_counts[key] += 1\n                seen.add(key)\n    result = []\n    for key, count in boot_means = np.array([arr[rng.randint(0, n, n)].mean() for _ in range(n_boot)])\n    boot_means.sort()\n    return {\n        \"mean\": float(arr.mean()),\n        \"ci_lo\": float(boot_means[int(0.025 * n_boot)]),\n        \"ci_hi\": float(boot_means[int(0.975 * n_boot)]),\n    }\n\n\ndef evaluate_dataset(name, cfg, thresholds, n_bootstrap=2000, dry_run=False):\n ### Assistant

_source line 58_

完成。结果总结：

**FewNERD** — consensus (high=0.5) 是唯一一个超越 greedy 的配置：**0.7583 vs 0.7478**（+1.05pp），也超过 LP selection (0.7666) 还差一点。高阈值损害性能严重（0.8→0.6780）。

**SciERC** — consensus (high=0.6) 接近 greedy（0.6444 vs 0.6439），但都不超过 LP selection (0.6501)。N=8 样本数有限，consensus 优势不明显。

**CoNLL** — greedy 最强 (0.9070)，consensus 全部配置都低于 greedy。该数据集 degeneracy 高（样本间差异小），consensus 无法提供增量。

**共性规律**：
- 阈值越高，F1 越低（保守 → 高 precision 但低 recall）
- LP-weighted medium zone 在低阈值时有正贡献
- Span-based majority vote 全面弱于 greedy，consensus 在 FewNERD 上有改善但幅度有限

输出文件：
- JSON: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/entity_consensus_results.json`
- Markdown: `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/entity_consensus_report.md`
- 脚本: `code/entity_consensus.py`
